CREATE TRIGGER tr_bi_check_uniqueness_with_nulls
BEFORE INSERT ON t1 FOR EACH ROW
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM t1 WHERE t1.id <=> NEW.id AND t1.ts <=> NEW.ts) THEN
    SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Duplicated values not allowed.';
  END IF;
END;

  parent_table INT,

  CONSTRAINT uk_constraints UNIQUE KEY uq_constraints (unique_col) USING BTREE COMMENT 'unique',
  CONSTRAINT fk_constraints FOREIGN KEY fk_parent (parent_table) REFERENCES split_table.parent(id) MATCH FULL ON UPDATE NO ACTION ON DELETE RESTRICT,

BEGIN
  DECLARE local_time TIME(6) DEFAULT TIME'00:00:00.000001';
  SET p_output=ADDTIME(p_input,local_time);

CREATE PROCEDURE datetime_parameters(IN p_datetime DATETIME(6),OUT p_timestamp TIMESTAMP(6),INOUT p_accumulator DATETIME(3))
BEGIN
  DECLARE local_datetime DATETIME(6) DEFAULT '2000-01-01 00:00:00.000001';
  DECLARE local_timestamp TIMESTAMP(6) DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6);
  SET p_timestamp=local_timestamp;
  SET p_accumulator=TIMESTAMPADD(MICROSECOND,1,p_datetime);
  SELECT local_datetime;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1

CREATE PROCEDURE p1()
BEGIN
  DECLARE dbl DOUBLE;
  DECLARE deci DECIMAL(10, 2);
  DECLARE intv INTEGER;
  DECLARE str VARBINARY(16);
  SELECT DISTINCT VAR_POP(1) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO dbl;
  SELECT DISTINCT STDDEV_POP(1) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO dbl;
  SELECT DISTINCT STDDEV_SAMP(1) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO dbl;
  SELECT DISTINCT AVG(3.14159) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO deci;
  SELECT DISTINCT AVG(3.14159E0) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO dbl;
  SELECT DISTINCT BIT_OR(x'0000') FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO str;
  SELECT DISTINCT BIT_OR(9) FROM proc_t1 GROUP BY pk INTO intv;
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE split_routine.proc_handler_conditions(INOUT p_seen INT)
outer_block: BEGIN
  DECLARE named_error CONDITION FOR SQLSTATE VALUE '45000';
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR 1062 SET p_seen = p_seen + 1;
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR SQLWARNING SET p_seen = p_seen + 10;
  inner_block: BEGIN
    DECLARE EXIT HANDLER FOR named_error SET p_seen = p_seen + 100;
    INSERT INTO split_routine.t VALUES (1, 'first');
    INSERT INTO split_routine.t VALUES (1, 'duplicate');
    SIGNAL named_error;
  END inner_block;
END outer_block;

[permission_mysql_8_0_00900]
source: split/mysql/5.6/ddl_mysql_stored_program_0.txt#3
sql:
CREATE PROCEDURE proc_compound_common(IN p_limit INT, OUT p_total INT)
COMMENT 'compound coverage'
LANGUAGE SQL
MODIFIES SQL DATA
SQL SECURITY INVOKER
BEGIN
  DECLARE v_id INT DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE v_done BOOL DEFAULT FALSE;
  DECLARE cur_ids CURSOR FOR SELECT id FROM proc_src ORDER BY id;
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET v_done = TRUE;
  SET p_total = 0;
  OPEN cur_ids;
  read_loop: LOOP
    FETCH NEXT FROM cur_ids INTO v_id;
    IF v_done THEN
      LEAVE read_loop;
    END IF;
    SET p_total = p_total + 1;
    CASE
      WHEN p_total < p_limit THEN SET v_id = v_id + 0;
      ELSE SET v_id = v_id;
    END CASE;
    IF p_total < p_limit THEN
      ITERATE read_loop;
    END IF;
  END LOOP read_loop;
  CLOSE cur_ids;
  WHILE p_total < p_limit DO
    SET p_total = p_total + 1;
  END WHILE;
  REPEAT
    SET p_total = p_total - 1;
  UNTIL p_total <= p_limit
  END REPEAT;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "DDL": [
    "Procedure(1:17~1:37) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/proc_compound_common/"
  ]
}
----------

CREATE FUNCTION fn_compound_common(p_value INT)
RETURNS INT
COMMENT 'function coverage'
LANGUAGE SQL
DETERMINISTIC
NO SQL
SQL SECURITY INVOKER
BEGIN
  DECLARE v_result INT DEFAULT 0;
  IF p_value IS NULL THEN
    SET v_result = 0;
  ELSE
    SET v_result = p_value + 1;
  END IF;
  RETURN v_result;
END;
levels:

[permission_mysql_8_0_00910]
source: split/mysql/5.6/ddl_mysql_stored_program_0.txt#14
sql:
CREATE EVENT split_event.ev_compound
ON SCHEDULE EVERY (1 + 1) DAY
STARTS CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + INTERVAL 1 HOUR
ENDS CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + INTERVAL 7 DAY
ON COMPLETION PRESERVE
DISABLE
COMMENT 'compound event'
DO event_block: BEGIN
  DECLARE done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
  DECLARE v_id INT;
  DECLARE cur CURSOR FOR SELECT id FROM split_event.event_log;
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET done = TRUE;
  OPEN cur;
  read_loop: LOOP
    FETCH cur INTO v_id;
    IF done THEN
      LEAVE read_loop;
    END IF;
    UPDATE split_event.event_log SET note = CONCAT('event-', v_id) WHERE id = v_id;
  END LOOP read_loop;
  CLOSE cur;
  CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM split_event.event_log WHERE id = 1) THEN
      INSERT INTO split_event.event_log VALUES (2, 'created') ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE note = VALUES(note);
    ELSE
      SET @event_case = 0;
  END CASE;
END event_block;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "MANAGE": [
    "Event(1:13~1:36) /test/1/catalog1/split_event/ev_compound/"
  ]
}
----------
[permission_mysql_8_0_00911]

source: split/mysql/5.6/ddl_mysql_stored_program_0.txt#36
sql:
CREATE PROCEDURE load_locale_format_table()
BEGIN
  DECLARE locale_list VARCHAR(1000) DEFAULT '
  es_AR,es_BO,es_CL,es_CO,es_CR,es_DO,es_EC,es_ES,es_GT,es_HN,
  es_MX,es_NI,es_PA,es_PE,es_PR,es_PY,es_SV,es_US,es_UY,es_VE';
  SET @fmt_stmt = 'INSERT INTO locale_format VALUES
                   (?, FORMAT(12131254123412541,2,?));';
  PREPARE stmt FROM @fmt_stmt;
  WHILE locale_list != '' DO
    SET @locale =
          TRIM(REPLACE((SUBSTRING_INDEX(locale_list, ',', 1)), '\n',''));
    EXECUTE stmt USING @locale, @locale;
    IF LOCATE(',', locale_list) > 0 THEN
      SET locale_list =
      SUBSTRING(locale_list, LOCATE(',', locale_list) + 1);
    ELSE
      SET locale_list = '';
    END IF;
  END WHILE;
  DEALLOCATE PREPARE stmt;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "DDL": [
    "Procedure(1:17~1:41) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/load_locale_format_table/"
  ]
}
----------

CREATE PROCEDURE p_diagnostics_full()
BEGIN
  DECLARE n INT DEFAULT 1;
  DECLARE t VARCHAR(128);
  DECLARE s CHAR(5);
  DECLARE e INT;
  GET CURRENT DIAGNOSTICS CONDITION n
    t=CLASS_ORIGIN, t=SUBCLASS_ORIGIN, s=RETURNED_SQLSTATE,
    t=MESSAGE_TEXT, e=MYSQL_ERRNO, t=CONSTRAINT_CATALOG,
    t=CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA, t=CONSTRAINT_NAME, t=CATALOG_NAME,
    t=SCHEMA_NAME, t=TABLE_NAME, t=COLUMN_NAME, t=CURSOR_NAME;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "DDL": [

CREATE PROCEDURE p_elseif(IN p INT, OUT o INT)
BEGIN
  IF p < 0 THEN SET o = -1;
  ELSEIF p = 0 THEN SET o = 0;
  ELSE SET o = 1;
  END IF;
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE p_signal_full()
BEGIN
  DECLARE c CONDITION FOR SQLSTATE '45000';
  SIGNAL c SET CLASS_ORIGIN='audit', SUBCLASS_ORIGIN='audit',
    MESSAGE_TEXT='full signal', MYSQL_ERRNO=45001,
    CONSTRAINT_CATALOG='def', CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA='audit_mysql_flow',
    CONSTRAINT_NAME='c1', CATALOG_NAME='def',
    SCHEMA_NAME='audit_mysql_flow', TABLE_NAME='t1',
    COLUMN_NAME='c1', CURSOR_NAME='cur1';
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE split_select_short.p1()
BEGIN
  DECLARE counter INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR SQLEXCEPTION BEGIN SET counter=counter+1; END;
  REPEAT
    IF RAND()>0.5 THEN START TRANSACTION; END IF;
    IF RAND()>0.5 THEN SELECT COUNT(*) FROM split_select_short.t FOR UPDATE; END IF;
    UPDATE split_select_short.t SET a=1 WHERE a>=0;
    SET counter=counter+1;
  UNTIL counter>=2 END REPEAT;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "DDL": [
    "Procedure(1:17~1:38) /test/1/catalog1/split_select_short/p1/"
  ]
}

CREATE PROCEDURE spatial_convert(
  IN input_geometry GEOMETRY,
  OUT output_point POINT,
  INOUT output_line LINESTRING
)
BEGIN
  DECLARE local_polygon POLYGON;
  SET output_point=ST_Centroid(input_geometry);
  SET output_line=ST_LineStringFromText('LINESTRING(0 0,1 1)');
  SET local_polygon=ST_PolygonFromText('POLYGON((0 0,0 1,1 1,0 0))');
END;
levels:

[permission_mysql_8_0_01016]
source: split/mysql/5.6/ddl_mysql_stored_program_0.txt#121
sql:
CREATE PROCEDURE integer_copy(
  IN tiny_input TINYINT,
  IN small_input SMALLINT,
  IN medium_input MEDIUMINT,
  IN int_input INT,
  IN big_input BIGINT,
  OUT big_output BIGINT UNSIGNED,
  INOUT bool_state BOOLEAN
)
BEGIN
  DECLARE tiny_local TINYINT DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE small_local SMALLINT DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE medium_local MEDIUMINT DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE int_local INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE big_local BIGINT DEFAULT 0;
  SET tiny_local=tiny_input;
  SET small_local=small_input;
  SET medium_local=medium_input;
  SET int_local=int_input;
  SET big_local=big_input;
  SET big_output=CAST(tiny_local+small_local+medium_local+int_local+big_local AS UNSIGNED);
  SET bool_state=NOT bool_state;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "DDL": [
    "Procedure(1:17~1:29) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/integer_copy/"
  ]

[permission_mysql_8_0_01020]
source: split/mysql/5.6/ddl_mysql_stored_program_0.txt#125
sql:
CREATE PROCEDURE numeric_copy(
  IN decimal_input DECIMAL(20,6),
  IN numeric_input NUMERIC(12,4),
  IN fixed_input FIXED(18,2),
  OUT float_output FLOAT,
  OUT double_output DOUBLE PRECISION,
  INOUT real_accumulator REAL
)
BEGIN
  DECLARE decimal_local DECIMAL(30,10) DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE numeric_local NUMERIC(20,8) DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE fixed_local FIXED(24,6) DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE float_local FLOAT DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE double_local DOUBLE PRECISION DEFAULT 0;
  DECLARE real_local REAL DEFAULT 0;
  SET decimal_local=decimal_input;
  SET numeric_local=numeric_input;
  SET fixed_local=fixed_input;
  SET float_local=decimal_local+numeric_local;
  SET double_local=fixed_local+float_local;
  SET real_local=real_accumulator+double_local;
  SET float_output=float_local;
  SET double_output=double_local;
  SET real_accumulator=real_local;
END;
levels:
/test/1/catalog1/schema1
expect:
{
  "DDL": [
    "Procedure(1:17~1:29) /test/1/catalog1/schema1/numeric_copy/"
  ]
}
